### [1] [AdaPilot: Towards Scene-Adaptive Policy Learning for Cross-Generator Text-to-Image Quality Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29517)
*Wenjin Liu,Fayuan Ke,Yue Lu,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaPilot，一种基于强化学习的通用文本到图像质量优化策略，通过解耦生成器内部机制实现跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如微调、提示优化、多轮视觉反馈强化学习）严重依赖特定生成器和任务，导致学到的能力难以泛化为通用的质量优化策略。

Method: 将多轮图像生成建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并通过端到端强化学习进行优化。具体包括：1. 策略与生成器内部解耦以实现跨生成器迁移；2. 引入场景感知奖励以动态对齐质量评估维度与任务语义；3. 采用过程级奖励来建模图像质量的演变轨迹。

Result: 在生成质量和泛化能力上优于基线方法；单一策略可实现零样本迁移至未见过的生成器，并在所有评估的生成器上保持平均正向增益。

Conclusion: AdaPilot成功构建了一个场景自适应且跨生成器可迁移的质量优化策略，解决了现有方法泛化性差的问题。

Abstract: Existing methods for improving text-to-image generation quality have progressed from generator fine-tuning and prompt optimization to reinforcement learning with multi-turn visual feedback. However, existing strategies are deeply coupled with specific generators and tasks, and the learned capabilities are difficult to generalize into a universal quality optimization policy. Therefore, we propose AdaPilot, which learns a scene-adaptive, cross-generator transferable quality optimization policy by formulating multi-turn image generation as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and optimizing it via end-to-end reinforcement learning. Specifically, AdaPilot decouples the policy from generator internals to enable cross-generator transfer, introduces scene-aware rewards that adaptively align quality assessment dimensions with task semantics, and employs process-level rewards to model the evolution trajectory of image quality. Experimental results show AdaPilot outperforms baselines in generation quality and generalization. Separate cross-generator evaluations further show that a single policy transfers zero-shot to unseen generators while maintaining positive average gains across all evaluated generators. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQing/Ada_pilot.

</details>


### [2] [Accelerating Video Diffusion via Training-Free Trajectory Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30096)
*Mustafa Munir,Huy Vu,Shreyas Misra,Rohit Jena,Sajad Norouzi,Ali Taghibakhshi,Anis Ahmad,Anjul Patney,Pavlo Molchanov,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRACK方法，通过在大/小模型间动态切换以加速视频扩散推理。利用校准阶段的大/小模型预测差异生成决策图，无需重训即可实现显著加速且保持质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型因需执行大量去噪步骤而计算昂贵，即便经过蒸馏，单次推理成本依然高昂。旨在解决这一效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出轨迹感知容量路由（TRACK）异构去噪策略。1. 使用大模型生成参考轨迹；2. 收集小模型在同一输入下的预测，计算相对分歧得分；3. 在校准集上聚合得分生成分歧地图，确定每步的模型选择策略；4. 高质量敏感步骤保留大模型，低分歧步骤路由至小模型。全程无需重训练或架构修改。

Result: 在Wan 2.1、Cosmos 3、TurboDiffusion和FastVideo上分别获得1.95倍、2.04-2.73倍、2.69倍和2.17倍的加速比，同时保持相当的聚合质量和多样性。

Conclusion: TRACK确立了一种自动化、免训练的模型切换范式，为视频扩散的高效推理提供了实用加速方案。

Abstract: Video diffusion is computationally expensive, as it requires executing a large model across many denoising steps. Even with step-distillation, inference remains expensive because every distilled step still requires a costly model evaluation. We present TRACK: TRajectory-Aware Capacity routing via top-K selection, a heterogeneous denoising strategy that switches between compatible large and small models at selected steps, reducing the average cost per denoising evaluation. The switching steps are determined using a calibration process. TRACK first rolls out a reference trajectory with the large model. Then at each step, the small model's prediction is also collected and compared against the large model's prediction to obtain a relative disagreement score. Both models receive the same latent, timestep, conditioning, and guidance inputs. Aggregating this signal over a calibration set produces a disagreement score map across diffusion steps, which determines a switching policy for an efficient inference process: quality-sensitive steps keep using the large model, while steps with low disagreement scores are routed to the small model. Inference executes only the selected model at each step, requiring no retraining, architecture or scheduler changes, or online dual-model evaluation. Across Wan 2.1, Cosmos 3, TurboDiffusion, and FastVideo, TRACK yields $1.95\times$, $2.04\times$-$2.73\times$, $2.69\times$, and $2.17\times$ speedups, respectively, with comparable aggregate quality and high diversity retention. TRACK thereby establishes automated, training-free model switching as a practical acceleration paradigm for video diffusion.

</details>


### [3] [The Alignment Illusion in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30210)
*Hong-Han Wang,Yuntao Wang,Hu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究揭示了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中层间视觉-文本相似性测量中的“对齐幻觉”现象，指出传统标量指标无法有效区分内容级跨模态交互与由语言模型路径引起的权重诱导对齐。为此，论文提出主角间隙（PA gap）作为更可靠的几何诊断工具，表明内部对齐应被视为视觉流的几何特征而非直接的内容交互代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为MLLMs中逐层的视觉-文本相似性是语言模型将视觉内容逐步整合到共享表示空间的证据，这一假设基于标量对齐分数反映内容级跨模态交互的前提。然而，该前提尚未得到充分验证，需要检验在视觉流受到干扰时，这些标量指标是否仍能准确反映模型状态。

Method: 通过对13个不同规模（0.5B至72B参数）的5种家族MLLMs进行受控干预，将投影器输出的视觉标记替换为高斯噪声以模拟视觉损坏。对比分析了四种标准标量测量方法（CKA, SVCCA, MIR, 主角余弦）与新提出的主角间隙（PA gap，即前两个主角余弦之差）在分离受损流与原始流、追踪任务准确率方面的表现。

Result: 1. 将视觉标记替换为高斯噪声后，任务准确率显著下降，但四种标准标量指标未能一致地区分受损流与原始流，这种现象被称为“对齐幻觉”。
2. “对齐幻觉”源于共享的语言模型路径：各向异性的MLP下投影将视觉和文本标记拉向共同的输出方向，产生权重诱导的对齐。
3. 由于该组件本质上是一维的，新提出的PA gap能有效分离权重诱导相似性与多方向视觉结构。
4. 在分级视觉损坏下，PA gap比考虑的标量分数更一致地追踪任务准确率；在结构化但无关的图像下，它进一步揭示了内部几何结构与任务准确率脱节的 regimes。

Conclusion: MLLMs内部的视觉-文本对齐不应被直接解读为内容级跨模态交互的代理，而应被视为语言模型内部视觉流的几何诊断工具。其信息价值最高时需结合受控的任务证据进行校准。

Abstract: Layer-wise visual-text similarity in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is widely interpreted as evidence that the language model progressively integrates visual content into a shared representation space. This reading rests on the assumption that scalar alignment scores reflect content-level cross-modal interaction. To test this assumption, we apply controlled interventions to the visual stream. Across 13 MLLMs from five families spanning 0.5B to 72B parameters, replacing projector-output visual tokens with Gaussian noise sharply reduces task accuracy, yet four standard scalar measures (CKA, SVCCA, MIR, and the leading principal-angle cosine) fail to consistently separate the corrupted stream from the original. We call this failure the alignment illusion and trace it to the shared language-model pathway: anisotropic MLP down-projections pull visual and text tokens toward common output directions, producing weight-induced alignment. Because this component is essentially one-dimensional, we introduce the principal-angle gap (PA gap), defined as the difference between the top two principal-angle cosines, which separates weight-induced similarity from multi-directional visual structure. Under graded visual corruption, the PA gap tracks task accuracy more consistently than the scalar scores we consider; under a structured but irrelevant image, it further exposes regimes in which internal geometry and task accuracy come apart. Internal visual-text alignment in MLLMs is therefore best read as a geometric diagnostic of the visual stream inside the language model rather than a direct proxy for content-level cross-modal interaction, and is most informative when calibrated by controlled task evidence.

</details>


### [4] [TrackEverything: Long Horizon Dense Tracking via De-Duplicating 3D Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30222)
*Ayush Jain,Sreeharsha Paruchuri,Ishita Gupta,Fan Zhang,Tanner Schmidt,Jakob Engel,Katerina Fragkiadaki,Adam W. Harley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrackEverything，首个能突破稀疏/长时序与密集/短片段权衡的3D点跟踪模型，实现千帧以上视频的全帧密集跟踪且显存占用低于40GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型面临根本性权衡：要么在长时间跨度上跟踪少量查询点，要么仅在短片段中跟踪所有点。需打破这一局限，实现高效、全帧的长视频3D点跟踪。

Method: 将视频表示为世界坐标系下的持久3D场景轨迹；引入基于体素化的去重机制合并重合轨迹；分解为终点细化器（预测目标位置及动静分类）和轻量级轨迹细化器（仅解码动态点轨迹）；提出3D WAFT替代高耗存的4D相关体积，通过场景云中的高效特征采样降低计算开销。

Result: 在TAPVid-3D数据集上，TrackEverything在短片段上的平均点距离误差（APD）比所有开源全帧密集3D跟踪器高出20%以上，同时在长序列上与最先进稀疏跟踪器表现相当，尽管其跟踪点数远超后者。

Conclusion: TrackEverything通过解耦模型复杂度与视频时长，实现了可扩展至物理场景几何结构的3D点跟踪，证明了在有限显存下对超长视频进行全帧密集跟踪的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Existing point tracking models face a fundamental tradeoff: they can either track a sparse set of query points over long horizons, or track all points across only short clips. We introduce TrackEverything, a 3D point tracker that breaks this trade-off by representing videos as persistent 3D scene tracks in world coordinates. Grounded in the insight that videos are 2D projections of an underlying 3D world, TrackEverything decouples model complexity from video duration, allowing it to scale with unique physical scene geometry instead. Our approach introduces three key innovations. First, we employ a voxelization-based de-duplication mechanism at sliding-window boundaries to merge co-located tracks, preventing repeated observations of the same surface from redundantly accumulating. Second, we decompose tracking into an endpoint refiner that predicts each point's destination and static-versus-dynamic classification, followed by a lightweight trajectory refiner that decodes dense trajectories exclusively for dynamic points. Third, we propose 3D WAFT, replacing memory-prohibitive 4D correlation volumes with efficient feature sampling in the scene cloud. To the best of our knowledge, TrackEverything is the first 3D tracker capable of tracking all visible points across videos exceeding 1000 frames within 40 GB of GPU memory. On TAPVid-3D, TrackEverything outperforms all open-source all-frame dense 3D trackers by more than 20% APD on short clips, while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art sparse trackers on long sequences, despite tracking far more points.

</details>

### [5] [Return or Revise? Learning When Revision Helps Retrieval-Augmented QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30087)
*Nicholas Kashani Motlagh,Tim Anderson,Jeremy Gwinnup,Grant Erdmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“可恢复性”（recoverability）概念来评估修订草稿答案的效果，并训练策略预测该值以优化答案修订决策，在多个设置下优于仅基于草稿置信度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在答案修订系统中，决定是返回现有草稿还是使用检索证据进行修订需要估计修订的影响，而不仅仅是评估当前答案的正确性。

Method: 提出“可恢复性”作为配对效应指标，通过在同一正确性评判标准下对草稿及其候选修订进行评分来观察修复、伤害及与理想情况的差距；据此训练策略模型预测修订效果。

Result: 在25,870个开放域问题上，基于配对结果的评分器在准确率-修订率曲线下面积优于草稿正确性评分器；平均提升0.23–0.68个百分点的准确率，关闭了超过三分之一的理想差距，但仍存在38–46%的有害修订。当提供无草稿的标准RAG答案时，比较草稿与该答案比考虑修订选项更有效。

Conclusion: 可恢复性是衡量单次修订价值的关键指标，但其作为可用行动的价值取决于可用的替代方案（如直接采用标准RAG答案）。

Abstract: We consider the decision of whether to return an existing draft answer or revise it using retrieved evidence, as in answer-revision systems. Draft confidence estimates whether the current answer is correct, but the decision requires estimating the effect of a specified revision. For offline training and evaluation, we grade both the returned draft and its candidate revision under the same correctness judge, which makes repair, harm, and the gap to an oracle observable. We call this paired effect its recoverability, and we train policies to predict it before revision. On 25,870 held-out open-domain questions across three revision setups, a scorer trained on the paired outcome has greater area under the accuracy--revision-rate curve than a matched draft-correctness scorer in all nine Llama setup--seed fits, and gains 0.23--0.68 accuracy points on average at development-selected thresholds, a difference significant across training runs only for dense retrieval. The resulting policy improves on always revising and on average closes more than a third of the oracle gap, although it still applies 38--46% of the harmful revisions. When a draft-free standard-RAG answer is also available, however, choosing between the draft and that answer is stronger by about two points for Llama and four for OLMo, and adding candidate revision as a third option yields no significant gain. Recoverability describes one revision; its value as an available action also depends on the alternatives.

</details>


### [6] [ModularSQL: A Runtime Guardrail for the Multiplicity Blind Spot in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29573)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出传统SQL生成评估指标Set-EX因忽略重复行而存在“多重性盲区”（MBS），导致实际执行结果失真。为此，作者提出保留多重性的Multiset-EX评估标准，并开发轻量级运行时护栏ModularSQL，通过检测异常并应用确定性修复或低成本LLM救援，以极小开销显著缩小基准准确率与实际执行安全性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Text-to-SQL系统在部署到生产数据库时，尽管能通过基准测试，但生成的查询可能导致下游工作流扭曲。传统的基于集合的执行准确率（Set-EX）会合并重复行，从而掩盖多重性错误（如缺失DISTINCT、聚合值膨胀和笛卡尔积式连接爆炸）。为了更准确地评估SQL生成的实际执行安全性，需要解决这一评估盲区。

Method: 1. 提出Multiset-EX：一种保留多重性的评估标准，用于暴露Set-EX无法捕捉的多重性错误。
2. 开发ModularSQL：一种轻量级的后选择运行时护栏，通过探测执行结果中的多重性异常，仅对标记的查询应用确定性补丁或低成本LLM救援。
3. 在DeepEye-SQL、DAIL-SQL+GPT-4等模型及BIRD-Dev数据集上进行跨管道评估。

Result: 1. 在多组实验中发现Set-EX与Multiset-EX之间存在5.81--6.79个百分点的稳定差距，证明基准准确率不等于执行安全。
2. ModularSQL集成Qwen3-Coder后，保持Set-EX为72.06%，将Multiset-EX从65.86%提升至67.75%（+1.89 pp）。
3. 该方案仅增加$0.0076的总LLM成本和120毫秒的平均延迟，即可识别77个高风险异常，且其检测方法可迁移至其他预测集。

Conclusion: 基准准确率并不保证SQL的执行安全性；引入轻量级、感知多重性的运行时护栏，可以在计算开销极低的情况下有效缩小这一差距，提升Text-to-SQL系统的生产可用性。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems are increasingly deployed on production databases, where queries that pass benchmark evaluation can still produce results that distort downstream workflows. Standard set-based execution accuracy (Set-EX) collapses duplicate rows and can therefore miss multiplicity errors, including missing DISTINCT, inflated aggregates, and Cartesian-style join explosions.
  We call this the Multiplicity Blind Spot (MBS) and introduce Multiset-EX, a multiplicity-preserving evaluation criterion that exposes such failures. Across released DeepEye-SQL artifacts from three backbones (Qwen2.5-Coder-32B, Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B, and Gemma-3-27B) on executable BIRD-Dev N=1532, we find a consistent 5.81--6.79 pp gap between Set-EX and Multiset-EX. The gap is not specific to DeepEye-SQL: it persists on released DAIL-SQL+GPT-4 (5.22 pp) and BIRD GPT-3.5-turbo (3.39 pp) predictions.
  We further introduce ModularSQL, a lightweight post-selection runtime guardrail that probes executed results for multiplicity anomalies and applies deterministic patches or low-cost LLM rescue only to flagged queries. Integrated with DeepEye-SQL using Qwen3-Coder, ModularSQL preserves Set-EX at 72.06% while improving Multiset-EX from 65.86% to 67.75% (+1.89 pp). It flags 77 high-risk anomalies, while adding only $0.0076 in total LLM cost and 120 ms amortized latency per query. Cross-pipeline evaluation shows that the candidate-free detector and deterministic patches also transfer to independently released prediction sets. Overall, these results show that benchmark accuracy does not necessarily imply execution-safe SQL, and that lightweight, multiplicity-aware runtime guardrails can narrow this gap with modest computational overhead.

</details>


### [7] [Stochastic Semantic Evidence Graphs: Uncertainty Propagation and Governance for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29703)
*Matthew Francis Dixon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出随机语义证据图（SSEG），将AI代理评估从单一最终答案检查扩展为对不确定性传播路径的定量分析，通过分层DAG结构追踪证据、检索和生成过程中的误差来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI代理评估仅关注最终答案，忽略了误差可能在证据获取、检索、提示工程、生成或决策映射等环节进入，导致无法准确诊断系统缺陷和不确定性来源。

Method: 构建层级随机有向无环图（SSEG），语言节点扩展为自回归token子图；定义图相对局部缺陷和下游边影响，推导终端误差的路径界；保留不确定声明-段落关系，使用Fréchet界限而非独立性假设进行源溯源。

Result: 在三种开源架构上验证，信息等效变化显著改变完整短语定律；5000例控制实验无证书违规；交叉RAG和实时Brave检索实验成功分离检索、呈现、源和交互效应。

Conclusion: SSEG将工作流溯源转化为对不确定性进入位置、传播方式及输出可用性的定量描述，提升了AI代理评估的可解释性和诊断能力。

Abstract: AI-agent evaluations usually inspect a final answer, yet error may enter through evidence, retrieval, prompting, generation or decision mapping. We introduce a stochastic semantic evidence graph (SSEG), a hierarchical stochastic DAG whose language node expands into an autoregressive token subgraph and whose observable output may be a law over complete phrases. Semantic reduction and calibration are optional. We define graph-relative local defects and downstream edge influences, derive a pathwise bound on terminal error and use its nodewise terms to diagnose governance triggers. For source provenance, the graph preserves uncertain claim--passage relations and propagates sharp Fréchet bounds rather than assuming independence across sources. Across three open-weight architectures, information-equivalent changes materially alter complete-phrase laws. A controlled experiment yields no certificate violations in 5,000 cases; crossed-RAG and live Brave-retrieval experiments separate retrieval, presentation, source and interaction effects. SSEG therefore turns workflow provenance into a quantitative account of where uncertainty entered, how it propagated and whether an output is qualified for use.

</details>


### [8] [TimeBraid: Unifying Time Series and Language for Understanding and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29792)
*Xinyue Wang,Jiacheng Pang,Kun Zhou,Kexin Zhang,Defu Cao,Fan Feng,Faisal,Songyao Jin,Yan Liu,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TimeBraid 是一种统一的时间序列与语言模型，通过交错的全局残差注意力层对齐预训练语言模型和时间序列基础模型。它结合了语言模型的推理/指令遵循能力与时间序列模型的感知/零样本预测能力，在共享表示空间中实现双向理解与生成。该模型设计解决了空间对齐、语言接地、平衡理解与生成及训练稳定性等关键问题，并在多个基准测试中展现出与更大规模通用模型及任务专用模型相媲美的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决单一模态模型无法同时具备强大语言推理能力和连续信号感知能力的局限，探索如何将预训练语言模型（PLM）的语义理解与预训练时间序列基础模型的时序建模能力进行有效融合，以实现跨模态的统一建模。

Method: 提出 TimeBraid 架构，核心创新在于使用“交错全局残差注意力层”（interleaved global residual attention layers）来对齐两种模态的表示空间。研究并确定了四个关键设计选择：1) 两个表示空间的对齐位置；2) 如何在时间结构中 grounding 语言；3) 如何平衡理解与生成任务；4) 如何保持联合优化的稳定性。采用统一的提示方案（unified prompting scheme），利用 2.2M 个精心策划的序列-文本对和 4.9M 个指令微调样本进行监督训练。

Result: TimeBraid 在涵盖时间序列感知、理解、推理以及上下文辅助和非单模态预测的多项基准测试中表现优异。尽管参数量远小于通用大模型或特定领域专用模型，TimeBraid 仍保持了具有竞争力的性能，证明了其统一建模方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过创新的架构设计和稳定的联合训练策略，TimeBraid 成功实现了时间序列与语言模态的有效融合。该方法不仅继承了各自模态的优势（如语言的推理能力和时序的零样本预测能力），还在共享空间中实现了高效的双向处理，为跨模态时间序列分析提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: We present TimeBraid, a series of unified time-series and language models that align pretrained language models and pretrained time-series foundation models through interleaved global residual attention layers. Each model inherits knowledge, instruction following, and reasoning from one side, continuous-signal perception and zero-shot forecasting from the other, and fuses the two in a shared representation space where both modalities are understood and generated. We study the design choices that make such unified modeling work: where to align the two representation spaces, how to ground language in temporal structure, how to balance understanding with generation, and how to keep joint optimization stable. The resulting recipe combines a unified prompting scheme for diverse time-series and text tasks, stabilized joint training, and supervision from 2.2M curated series--text pairs and 4.9M instruction-tuning samples. Across benchmarks spanning time-series perception, understanding, reasoning, and both context-aided and unimodal forecasting, TimeBraid remains competitive with far larger general-purpose models and task-specific counterparts.

</details>


### [9] [An Empirical Study of VLM Pipelines for Long-Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29933)
*Kenan E. Ak,Jay Mohta,Gwang Gook Lee,Yan Xu,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究在长文档处理中如何部署视觉语言模型（VLMs），比较了静态输入、检索策略和智能体模式。发现智能体仅在大型VLM上表现优异，图像检索比文本检索更高效且效果相当，且不同管道适用于不同类型的问题，最佳组合策略可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLMs在长文档处理中的应用增加，面临输入格式选择（如页面、图表、表格）、检索器使用以及运行模式（智能体vs静态管道）的决策问题。需要明确这些选择在基准测试中的实际效能差异。

Method: 在两个长文档问答基准（MMLongBench-Doc和LongDocURL）上，评估前沿API和开源VLMs。对比了六种工具的智能体架构与静态页面输入，分析了不同模态（图像vs文本）的检索策略及其效率，并评估了基于证据类型的路由策略对混合管道选择的增益。

Result: 1. 智能体优势依赖模型规模：在小模型（Qwen3.5-4B/9B）上落后于静态输入，中等模型持平，仅在最先进模型（Sonnet 4.5）上领先。2. 检索模态优于具体检索器：最强图像检索器胜过最强文本管道；轻量级交叉编码器重排序接近重型多阶段LLM流水线。3. 图像检索最节省Token（仅为全页输入的1/7至1/4）。4. 不同管道擅长不同问题，Oracle最佳选择策略比单一最佳管道提升约13分，但基于证据的路由几乎无增益。

Conclusion: 部署VLM进行长文档处理时，没有单一的“最佳”配置。智能体模式需配合大规模模型才能发挥优势；图像检索在效率和性能上具有显著竞争力；由于任务特异性，动态选择或混合使用不同管道策略能带来最大性能提升，而简单的证据路由无法有效实现这一目标。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used for long-document processing, where the inputs combine text with charts, tables, figures, and complex layouts. Deploying them means choosing how to feed the document to the model, which retriever to use when only a subset of pages is sent, and whether to run the model agentically or as a static pipeline. We study these choices on two long-document QA benchmarks with both frontier API and open-weight VLMs. First, on MMLongBench-Doc our six-tool agent with page, table, figure, and search calls pays off only once the answering VLM is large enough: with Qwen3.5-4B and 9B it trails static page input, with Qwen3.5-27B it draws level, and with Sonnet 4.5 it leads. On LongDocURL it is level with or ahead of static input at every reader. Its lead over the strongest static pipeline is clearest with the frontier reader on MMLongBench-Doc and narrows to within noise on LongDocURL. Second, retrieval modality matters more than the specific retriever: the strongest image retriever leads the strongest text pipeline, and on the text side a single off-the-shelf cross-encoder rerank essentially matches a much heavier multi-stage LLM pipeline. Top-k image retrieval is also the most token-efficient input at every reader we paired it with, at roughly a seventh to a quarter of the tokens of sending every page. Third, cutting across all three choices, three of our strongest pipelines succeed on different questions, and an oracle that picks the best pipeline per question gains roughly thirteen points over the best single pipeline, though evidence-type routing recovers almost none of it.

</details>


### [10] [R-DEIM Net: An Efficient Rationale-Augmented Dual-Expert Interaction Model for Paraphrase Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30100)
*Pushp,Vaibhav Prajapati,Himangshu Sarma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出R-DEIM Net，一种76M参数的双专家架构，在保持与大型模型相当准确率的同时，通过生成人类可读的解释来增强透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有方法中高精度大语言模型计算成本高与高效Siamese-BERT变体缺乏可解释性之间的权衡问题。

Method: 构建包含交互专家（捕捉多尺度2D卷积和注意力模式的token级相似性）和推理专家（使用Flan-T5-small解码器生成辅助监督理由）的双专家结构；分类时提取并池化解码器隐藏状态而非重编码文本。

Result: 在Quora Question Pairs数据集上达到90.07%准确率和90.16% F1分数，性能媲美MFAE BERT等大模型基线，且参数量显著更少。

Conclusion: 中等规模模型可在保证竞争力的同时实现可解释的推理生成，为平衡性能、效率与透明度提供了新途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in paraphrase detection reveal a fundamental trade-off: large language models achieve high accuracy but require high computation, while efficient Siamese-BERT variants offer practical scalability with reduced transparency in rationale generation. We present R-DEIM Net, a 76M-parameter dual-expert architecture exploring whether moderate-scale models can achieve competitive accuracy on paraphrase detection while enabling human-readable rationale generation. The architecture combines two specialized components: an Interaction Expert that captures token-level similarity patterns through multi-scale 2D convolutions and attention head allowing variable input length, and a Reasoning Expert that uses a Flan-T5-small decoder to generate rationales as auxiliary supervision. Rather than re-encoding generated text, we extract and pool decoder hidden states as complementary features for classification. On the Quora Question Pairs dataset, R-DEIM Net achieves 90.07\% accuracy and 90.16\% F1-score via 10-fold cross-validation. This represents competitive performance with strong transformer-based baselines (e.g., MFAE BERT: 90.54\% accuracy) and recent large language model based approaches (LLaMA-70B) while using a substantially smaller parameter budget. The model generates rationales alongside predictions, providing potential for auxiliary human-readable descriptions.

</details>


### [11] [Does a model's stated reason for rejecting a candidate do any work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30151)
*Archit Rastogi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过干预语言模型的推理过程，发现当向被模型拒绝的候选者补充其声称缺失的事实时，模型更有可能改变选择。然而，这种效应部分源于文本位置而非内容本身，且存在解析缺陷导致的测量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证语言模型在解释选择理由时提出的“事实缺失”主张是否真实反映其内部知识状态，以及这些主张是否具有可测试性。

Method: 在2WikiMultihopQA数据集上，使用6个开源模型进行三轮实验。采用贪婪解码生成初始选择及理由。随后，将被模型指出缺失的事实插入该候选者的资料中，并设置控制组（无关句子、不同位置）以分离内容与位置的影响。通过对比干预前后的选择变化及单令牌概率读数进行分析。

Result: 1. 在被选中的对手处补充缺失事实显著提高了模型改变选择的几率（优势比3.57，p=0.0210）。2. 相同事实出现在未被提及的第三个选项时无显著影响（p=0.2428）。3. 最强结果其实无关内容：无关句子放在被指出的对手处也比放在第三选项更能引起选择改变（p=0.0008），表明位置效应的存在。4. 强制单令牌概率读数与自由文本选择在方向上不一致，且现有解释无法支持这一差异。5. 数据验证发现8个缺陷，其中最大的解析错误导致高估了显著对比的数量。

Conclusion: 语言模型基于“事实缺失”的解释具有一定的可测试性和真实性，但其对选择的影响不仅由内容决定，还受到文本放置位置的显著干扰。此外，自动解析存在严重误差，需谨慎对待基于文本生成的评估结果。

Abstract: Asked to choose between candidates and explain the choice, a language model often rejects a rival by naming a fact its profile lacks: no director, no date of death. That sentence is a claim about the text in front of the model, and it can be tested without any judge. We insert a real corpus sentence stating the named fact into the rival's profile and ask again under greedy decoding. Two controls separate content from placement: a length-matched irrelevant sentence at the same profile, and the same two sentences at a third option the model never mentioned. In the largest of three runs, six open models on 2WikiMultihopQA, supplying the named fact at the profile the model named moves its choice more than the irrelevant control does, odds ratio 3.57 [1.54, 8.26], Holm p=0.0210, and this survives dropping any single model. The contrast the design was built to detect, the same fact at the option nobody named, does not clear correction, Holm p=0.2428. The strongest result in the family carries no content claim at all: the identical irrelevant sentence moves the choice more at the named rival than at the third option, Holm p=0.0008. Repair and control also differ in co-candidate mentions, relation template and fluency; post-hoc matching on the first two preserves the content effects' direction, matching fluency weakens one, so the content contrasts bound an effect rather than establish one. A forced single-token probability read disagrees in direction with the free-text choice on that same contrast, and three candidate explanations for the disagreement find no support. Every measurement is a string rule, so each was validated against the records it reads; validation caught eight defects. The largest, a choice-parsing rule that returned the option a model had just rejected in 17.1% of adjudicable responses, would have reported six surviving contrasts instead of four.

</details>


### [12] [Do Audio Language Models Hear and Read Distinctive Features Alike?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30167)
*Yuanhao Chen,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究语音语言模型中解码器是否以相同方向表征语音和文本中的音位特征。通过比较最小对立对的平均偏移量方向余弦，发现仅Qwen2.5-Omni模型的浊音特征显著超出随机参考基准，且模型家族而非大小决定特征表征方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究音频语言模型（同时处理语音和文本）的单一解码器是否在感知语音和阅读文本时，对音位特征（如浊音）采用一致的方向性表征。

Method: 针对6个模型、7种特征和15种语言的11个语系，选取最小对立对（minimal pairs），计算两个成员平均表示之间的偏移量作为各流方向，测量两流间的余弦相似度。由于任意音位对已存在一致性，故使用随机配对构建的参考基准进行比较，而非零值。

Result: 除Qwen2.5-Omni模型中的浊音特征经多重检验校正后显著高于参考基准外，其他特征未显著超越。不同模型间参考基准差异达7倍。在三个模型中，浊音在14种语言中呈现单一方向，且语言对在其中两个模型中达成一致。

Conclusion: 模型架构家族（model family）是预测哪个流表征特定特征的关键因素，而模型规模（size）并非决定性因素。

Abstract: Audio language models pass speech and text through a single decoder. We ask whether that decoder represents a distinctive feature in the same direction when a phoneme is heard and when it is read. For minimal pairs of phonemes differing in one feature, we take the offset between the two members' mean representations. Averaging those offsets gives a direction for each stream, and we measure the cosine between the two. Because the two streams already agree about arbitrary phoneme pairs, we compare every measure against a reference built from random pairings rather than against zero. We apply this to 6 models, 7 features and 15 languages from 11 families. Only voicing in the two Qwen2.5-Omni models exceeds that reference after correction for multiple testing, and the reference varies by a factor of seven between models. In three of the six models, voicing has one direction in audio across the 14 languages with enough minimal pairs to measure it, and every language pair agrees in two of them. The model family, not the model size, predicts which stream represents a feature.

</details>


### [13] [Agentic Detection of Online Conspiracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30250)
*Lior Biton,Oren Tsur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于社会语境和智能体（Agentic）框架的阴谋论检测模型，通过动态查询相关社会背景来推断发言者意图，显著优于仅依赖文本分类的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的阴谋论话语往往不通过明确的声明或稳定的词汇标记表达，同一表面内容可能蕴含支持、批评、讽刺等多种意图。主要挑战在于识别发言者的言语行为力（illocutionary force），即推断其真实意图，而不仅仅是识别阴谋论主张本身。

Method: 构建一个配备社会查询工具的代理框架（agentic framework）。该框架利用广泛的社交语境数据，使智能体能够根据当前推理步骤自适应地调用工具，仅获取与当前推理相关的证据，从而实现对隐含意图的上下文感知分析。

Result: 在涵盖2018-2023年希伯来语推文的大型数据集上进行了评估。结果显示：1. 上下文感知的工作流程始终优于纯文本分类；2. 智能体框架的表现显著优于其他框架及设置（包括暴露于相同语境的非智能体模型）；3. 提供了对结果、错误分析及效率（Token经济）权衡的详细分析。

Conclusion: 阴谋论检测应被视为一种嵌入社会的解释任务。有效的分类不仅依赖于获取语境信息，更取决于智能体的自适应推理能力，即能够按需使用工具进行针对性取证，而非静态地处理所有可用信息。

Abstract: Conspiratorial discourse on social media is not always expressed through explicit claims or stable lexical markers. The same surface content may express endorsement, legitimate concerns, criticism, satire, or mockery. The main challenge is therefore not only recognizing conspiracy-related claims, but inferring the speaker's intent -- the utterance's illocutionary force. We argue that this can be achieved through the use of relevant social contexts and propose an agentic framework, equipped with a set of tools supporting social queries.
  We demonstrate the benefits of our approach on a unique dataset of Hebrew tweets, covering 80\%--90\% of the public Hebrew tweets published over a four-year span (late 2018-- early 2023), encompassing several election cycles as well as the COVID pandemic years and related vaccination campaigns. This extensive coverage can be used in recovering different social contexts. Evaluating our framework on a manually-annotated adversarial dataset, we find that context-aware workflows consistently outperform text-only classification and that the agentic framework performs significantly better than other frameworks and settings, including a non-agentic model exposed to the same contexts available to the agent. We further provide an analysis of the results, the errors and efficiency (token economy) tradeoffs.
  These findings support viewing the task of conspiracy detection as a socially embedded interpretation task, in which effective classification depends not only on access to contexts, but also on adaptive reasoning in which the agent uses tools on a per-case basis, asking only for evidence relevant to its current reasoning step.

</details>


### [14] [IterSynth: Rethinking Deep Search Agents via Role-Decoupled Iterative Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29444)
*Xingyu Wu,Yuchen Yan,Zhengxi Lu,Siqi Chen,Xin ZHANG,Aiting Liu,Chao Deng,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Jian Shao,Jun Xiao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IterSynth，一种解耦角色和基于摘要的搜索范式，结合RDPO训练方法，在长程深度搜索任务中显著超越现有8B以下模型及提示范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReAct风格智能体存在角色耦合（单一策略需处理规划、证据使用和合成）和上下文累积（增长的历史记录引入噪声并掩盖有用信息）两大局限性。

Method: 1. 提出IterSynth范式：交替使用Planner（识别信息需求）和Synthesizer（将证据整合到演进式摘要状态中），通过摘要作为持久状态减少能力耦合和上下文噪声。2. 引入角色解耦策略优化（RDPO）进行强化学习训练，结合终端结果奖励、回合级评估标准和角色特定优势计算以实现精确信用分配。

Result: 在BrowseComp和Xbench-DS等五个长程深度搜索基准测试上，IterSynth-8B平均得分50.7，超越最强前序≤8B智能体4.2%。此外，该范式对前沿专有模型具有零样本增益效果。

Conclusion: IterSynth通过解耦角色和基于摘要的状态管理有效解决了深度搜索中的耦合与噪声问题，RDPO训练方法提升了性能，且该范式具有模型无关性，能显著提升不同规模模型的搜索能力。

Abstract: Deep search requires LLM agents to decompose complex queries, search for evidence, and synthesize grounded answers, yet existing ReAct-style agents suffer from two limitations: role coupling, where one policy must handle planning, evidence use, and synthesis; and context accumulation, where growing search histories introduce noise and obscure useful information. To address these issues, we propose IterSynth, a role-decoupled and summary-based paradigm that alternates between a Planner for identifying information needs and a Synthesizer for integrating evidence into an evolving summary state. This design separates planning from synthesis while using the summary as the persistent state of search, reducing both capability coupling and context noise. To train IterSynth effectively, we further introduce Role-Decoupled Policy Optimization (RDPO) for reinforcement learning, which combines terminal outcome rewards with turn-level rubric evaluations and computes role-specific advantages for more precise credit assignment. Experiments on five long-horizon deep-search benchmarks such as BrowseComp and Xbench-DS show that IterSynth-8B achieves an average score of 50.7, surpassing the strongest prior $\leq$8B agent by +4.2\%. Moreover, IterSynth serves as a model-agnostic prompting paradigm, delivering substantial zero-shot gains over ReAct and similar prompting paradigms on frontier proprietary models.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [15] [SEEK: Skill-Routed Evaluation with Evolvable Knowledge for Industrial Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29803)
*Zhongxin Huang,Songyang Li,Renzhe Zhou,Feiran Zhu,Chenglei Dai,Zhen Xiao,Xuanping Li,Jingwei Zhuo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SEEK方法，通过技能路由和可进化知识库解决大模型搜索评估中的标准干扰与更新成本问题，已在快手大规模部署并显著提升评估效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评估面临挑战：用户以页面级体验搜索结果，但评估标准多维且动态变化；统一提示词引入无关上下文和标准干扰，而微调训练导致规则更新需高昂的重训成本。

Method: 提出Skill-routed Evaluation with Evolvable Knowledge (SEEK)：将评估标准外化为技能库，针对每个查询-结果对动态路由相关技能，使用任务适配的列表级评估器生成页面级判断及故障归因；采用两阶段训练对齐人类偏好，并通过重放门控技能库在无需重训的情况下纳入新的评估知识。

Result: 在工业短视频搜索实验中，SEEK提高了列表级质量评估的准确性，并在故障归因诊断方面取得显著进展。

Conclusion: SEEK已成功部署于拥有4亿日活用户的快手平台，显著提升了在线搜索评估的规模和质量。

Abstract: Search quality evaluation provides essential supervision and diagnostic signals for the development and iteration of industrial search systems. Although large language models (LLMs) offer a scalable alternative to manual assessment, reliable automatic evaluation remains challenging: users experience search results at the page level, while the applicable evaluation criteria are multi-dimensional and continuously evolving. Packing all evaluation criteria into a unified prompt introduces irrelevant context and potential criterion interference, whereas internalizing them through post-training tightly couples rule updates with costly model retraining cycles.
  To address these issues, we propose Skill-routed Evaluation with Evolvable Knowledge (SEEK). Specifically, SEEK externalizes specific search evaluation criteria into a skill bank, dynamically routes relevant skills for each query-result list pair, and employs a task-adapted listwise evaluator to produce page-level judgments and failure mode attribution. A two-stage training pipeline teaches the evaluator to align evaluation criteria with human preferences, while a replay-gated skill bank allows recurring evaluation knowledge gaps to be incorporated without model retraining. Experiments on industrial short-video search show that SEEK improves listwise quality evaluation accuracy and achieves significant progress in attribution diagnosis. SEEK has been deployed at Kuaishou, a short-video platform with over 400 million daily active users, significantly improving the scale and quality of online search evaluation.

</details>
